# Accuracy: app vs ffprobe

Generated by `npm run compare` (`scripts/compare-ffprobe.ts`) on Node v24.20.0. ffprobe references: ffprobe version 8.1.1-full_build-www.gyan.dev. Timings are the median of 3 run(s) in Node, not in a browser; reads and bytes are unique reads through the shared reader.

Verdicts: **exact** (equal under the research 6.4 rule), **within-tol** (inside the stated tolerance), **known-deviation** (differs from ffprobe, with a recorded reason), **mismatch** (unexpected), **n/a** (the rule does not apply). Only the `own` engine fails the run on a mismatch; library engines are informational.

Every mismatch has one outcome: **wrong** (the arm returns a value that differs from ffprobe, and neither the arm's own definition nor another checked one explains it), **definition differs** (the value follows the definition the library documents or implements for the field, evaluated on the file and its reference: a float instead of the exact rational, mediabunny's frame rate metrics over its probe window, the movie or media header duration that mp4box.js reports, an end timestamp instead of a span), **unverified** (the arm's own definition cannot be evaluated on the file, or the value equals another quantity that the arm does not use for the field; counted neither as wrong nor as a definition difference), **not provided** (the arm returns no value for the field, or the field is not part of its recipe), **unsupported container** (the library does not read the container).

mp4box 2.4.1, idiomatic: README recipe: createFile(), appendBuffer() of 1 MiB chunks at the offset it asks for until onReady, then its info fields (codec, video width and height, nb_samples, track and movie duration over timescale); no frame rate, by design.

### mp4box-idiomatic: deviations and mismatches

| Fixture | Field | App | ffprobe | Verdict or outcome | Reason or rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| cfr_h264_23.976.mp4 | avgFps | - | 24000/1001 | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. the app reported no average. rule: avgFpsFfprobe (the duration-sum rational) must equal avg_frame_rate exactly |
| cfr_h264_23.976.mp4 | nominalFps | - | 24000/1001 | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. the reference has a single PTS delta (CFR), so the app must report an exact nominal rate. rule: Exact vs r_frame_rate, only when the track is CFR (stage 1); otherwise skipped |
| cfr_h264_23.976.mp4 | presentedFrames | - | 72 | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. the app reported no presented frame count. rule: Exact vs nb_read_frames |
| cfr_h264_23.976.mp4 | startSec | - | 0.000000 | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. the app reported no start time. rule: Exact vs start_pts x time_base |
| cfr_h264_23.976.mp4 | minFps | - | 24000/1001 | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. rule: Exact rational vs the tail-excluded reference minimum |
| cfr_h264_23.976.mp4 | maxFps | - | 24000/1001 | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. rule: Exact rational vs the tail-excluded reference maximum |
| cfr_h264_23.976.mp4 | isVfr | - | false | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. rule: Equal to the reference VFR flag (shared rule, tolerance max(1 tick, 0.5% of the median delta)) |
| cfr_vp9_23.976.webm | error | error: mp4box found no moov | opens as matroska,webm, 1 video stream(s) | unsupported-container | the library reads MP4 and MOV only. rule: Outcome equals the manifest expectation (manifest expect=ok) |
| cfr_vp9_23.976.webm | trackSelection | no primary track, 0 track(s) | stream 0 of 1: vp9 160x90 | unsupported-container | the library reads MP4 and MOV only. app lists 0 video tracks, ffprobe 1; app has no primary track. rule: Same real video tracks and primary track as ffprobe (attached_pic excluded; MP4/MOV: enabled tracks only) |
| cfr_vp9_23.976.webm | formatDurationSec | - | 3.003000 | unsupported-container | the library reads MP4 and MOV only. the app reported no format duration. rule: Exact vs format.duration when Info Duration exists; ffprobe N/A is not compared |
| cfr_h264_24.mp4 | avgFps | - | 24/1 | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. the app reported no average. rule: avgFpsFfprobe (the duration-sum rational) must equal avg_frame_rate exactly |
| cfr_h264_24.mp4 | nominalFps | - | 24/1 | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. the reference has a single PTS delta (CFR), so the app must report an exact nominal rate. rule: Exact vs r_frame_rate, only when the track is CFR (stage 1); otherwise skipped |
| cfr_h264_24.mp4 | presentedFrames | - | 72 | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. the app reported no presented frame count. rule: Exact vs nb_read_frames |
| cfr_h264_24.mp4 | startSec | - | 0.000000 | not-provided | not part of the arm's recipe. the app reported no start time. rule: Exact vs start_pts x time_base |
